7631	The method `getServerFeatures` retrieves server features from a specified directory, including configuration dropins and server XML files. It first retrieves features from the default configuration directory and then adds features from the server XML file. The overrides are added at the end to ensure they are not replaced by any previous content.
7632	This method retrieves server features from a specified directory, sorting them alphabetically and merging them with existing features.
7633	The method `getServerXmlFeatures` processes a server XML file to extract and return a set of features. It first retrieves the canonical file path of the server XML, then parses the XML to extract feature-related nodes. If the feature manager node is found, it adds the features to the result set. If the include node is found, it recursively parses the included XML files and adds the features to the result set. If the server XML file cannot be accessed or parsed, it logs a warning and skips the file.
